     def anonymize(content):
         content = re.sub(r'caoxiaoyuyuyuyuyu', 'anonymous', content, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
         content = re.sub(r'caoxiaoyu', 'anonymous', content, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
         content = content.replace('1844118046@qq.com', 'anonymous@example.com')
         for domain in ['qq.com', 'gmail.com', '163.com', '126.com', 'hotmail.com', 'outlook.com', 'yahoo.com']:
             escaped = domain.replace('.', r'\.')
             content = re.sub(r'[a-zA-Z0-9_.+-]+@' + escaped, 'anonymous@example.com', content)
         content = re.sub(r'(wandb\.init\s*\([^)]*entity\s*=\s*)["\'][^"\']*["\']', r'\1"anonymous"', content)
         for inst in ['清华', 'Tsinghua', '北大', 'PKU', '复旦', 'Fudan', '上海交通大学', '浙江大学', '中国科学院', 'Peking University']:
             content = content.replace(inst, '[University]')
         lines = content.split('\n')
         filtered = []
         for line in lines:
             stripped = line.strip()
             if re.match(r'^#\s*(Author|Copyright|Written by|Created by|Maintainer)\s*[:：]', stripped, re.IGNORECASE):
                 continue
             if re.match(r'^#\s*©', stripped):
                 continue
             filtered.append(line)
         return '\n'.join(filtered)
